<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">correctionNeeded; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// => [1, 1, 3]</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li><strong>Symbols as WeakMap keys</strong></li>
</ul>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-symbols-as-weakmap-keys">tc39/proposal-symbols-as-weakmap-keys</a></p>
<p>제목에서 파악이 가능하듯이 WeakMap에 키에 Symbol을 넣을 수 있습니다. 2022까지는 문자(string)만 넣을 수 있었습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> weak</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> WeakMap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Pun not intended: being a symbol makes it become a more symbolic key</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Symbol</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'my ref'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> someObject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  /* data data data */</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">weak.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(key, someObject);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>Hashbang Grammar</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-hashbang">tc39/proposal-hashbang</a></p>
<p>Javascript 파일이 CLI에서 실행될 경우 HashBang(#!) 기호와 해당 라인이 무시됩니다. 단, 소소코드의 시작 부분에만 유효합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">#!/usr/bin/env node</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// in the Script Goal</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'use strict'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">#</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">usr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">bin</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">env node</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// in the Module Goal</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es2022es13">ES2022(ES13)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2022es13"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>ES2022에 추가된 기능들
<a href="https://tc39.es/ecma262/">https://tc39.es/ecma262/</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Class Fields</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>babel 세팅</p>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npm install </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">save</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">dev @babel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">plugin</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">proposal</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">private</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">property</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-in-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">object</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">webpack.config.js</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  "plugins"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"@babel/plugin-proposal-private-property-in-object"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// babel.config.json</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  "assumptions"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    "privateFieldsAsProperties"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    "setPublicClassFields"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Class Public Instance Fields &#x26; Private Instance Fields</li>
</ul>
<p>ES2015 이후로, 우리는 생성자를 통해 필드를 정의할 수 있었습니다. 일반적으로 클래스 메서드 외부에서 액세스하면 안 되는 필드에는 밑줄이 붙습니다. 하지만 이는 클래스를 사용하는 사람들을 막지 못했습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ES2022 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.size </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> extends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    super</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.color </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'red'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">._clicked </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ColorButton</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Public fields can be accessed and changed by anyone</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">button.color </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'blue'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(button._clicked);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">button._clicked </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// noop!!</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 이 기능의 첫번째 부분은 클래스 내의 필드를 좀 더 명확하게 정의할 수 있게 해줍니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 생성자 내에 정의하는 대신, 클래스의 최상단 레벨에 정의할 수 있습니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 두번째 부분은, private 필드를 좀 더 안전하게 숨길 수 있습니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 밑줄을 붙이는 기존의 방식과 달리 필드 이름 앞에 '#'을 붙여 외부의 액세스를 방지할 수 있습니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  #size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> extends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  color</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'red'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  #clicked</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">flag</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#clicked </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  getClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#clicked;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Private instance methods and accessors</li>
</ul>
<p>클래스의 몇몇 메소드나 변수는 클래스 내부적으로 기존에 의도했던 기능들을 수행해야 하는 중요도를 가지면서 외부에서 접근할 수 없어야 합니다. 이를 방지하기 위해, 메소드나 접근자 앞에 '#'을 붙일 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Banner</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> extends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> HTMLElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Private variable that cannot be reached directly from outside, but can be modified by the methods inside:</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  #slogan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> "Hello there!"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  #counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // private getters and setters (accessors):</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">slogan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #slogan.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toUpperCase</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">slogan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#slogan </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> text.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">trim</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #counter}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#counter </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> value}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    super</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.onmouseover </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#mouseover.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">bind</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // private method:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">mouseover</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#counter </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#slogan </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> `Hello there! You've been here ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">#counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">} times.`</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Static class fields and private static methods</li>
</ul>
<p>정적 필드나 메소드는 프로토타입 내에서만 존재하도록 하는 데 있어 유용하지만, 주어진 클래스의 모든 인스턴스에 대해서는 그렇지 않습니다. 당신은 이 필드와 메소드들이 클래스 내에서만 액세스할 수 있도록 허용할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Circle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // ES2015 이후로, 우리는 필드를 클래스 자체에 정의함으로서 정적 필드를 정의했습니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  static</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> #PI</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3.14</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  static</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">calculateArea</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">radius</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">PI</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> radius </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> radius</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  static</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> calculateProperties</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">radius</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      radius: radius,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      area: #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">calculateArea</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(radius)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Public static method, outputs {radius: 10, area: 314}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Circle.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">calculateProperties</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">))</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// SyntaxError - Private static field</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Circle.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">PI</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// SyntaxError - Private static method</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Circle.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">calculateArea</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">))</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Ergonomic brand checks for Private Fields</li>
</ul>
<p>public 필드에 대해, 클래스의 존재하지 않는 필드에 접근을 시도하면 <code>undefined</code>가 반환됩니다. 반면에, private 필드는 <code>undefined</code>대신 예외를 발생시킵니다.</p>
<p>이를 방지하기 위해 <code>in</code> 키워드를 사용해 private 속성/메소드를 체크할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> VeryPrivate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    super</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  #variable</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">method</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.#variable </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> text;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  static</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> isPrivate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">obj</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      #variable </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> obj </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #method </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> obj </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #getter </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> obj </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> #setter </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> obj</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> VeryPrivate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(VeryPrivate.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">isPrivate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(b));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>RegExp Match Indices</li>
</ul>
<p>원본 입력에서 전체 일치의 위치에 대해 꽤 많은 정보를 제공하지만 하위 문자열 일치의 인덱스에 대한 정보는 부족합니다. 새로운 <code>/d</code> 를 사용하면, 일치한 그룹에 대해 시작, 끝 위치를 얻을 수 있습니다</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v2022 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Ingredients: cocoa powder, cocoa butter, other stuff'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> regex</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /(cocoa) (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">[a-z]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">)/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">g</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> matches</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">str.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">matchAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(regex)];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0: "cocoa powder", 1: "cocoa", 2: "powder"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// index: 13</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// input: "Ingredients: cocoa powder, cocoa butter, other stuff"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(matches[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0: "cocoa butter", 1: "cocoa", 2: "butter"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// index: 27</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// input: "Ingredients: cocoa powder, cocoa butter, other stuff"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(matches[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// vs2022 이후</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Ingredients: cocoa powder, cocoa butter, other stuff'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> regex</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /(cocoa) (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">[a-z]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">)/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">dg</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> matches</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">str.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">matchAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(regex)];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0: "cocoa powder", 1: "cocoa", 2: "powder"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// index: 13</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// input: "Ingredients: cocoa powder, cocoa butter, other stuff"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// indices: [[13,25],[13,18],[19,25]]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(matches[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0: "cocoa butter", 1: "cocoa", 2: "butter"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// index: 27</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// input: "Ingredients: cocoa powder, cocoa butter, other stuff"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// indices: [[27,39],[27,32],[33,39]]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(matches[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Top-level await</li>
</ul>
<p>이 기능 이전에는, await은 오직 async 함수 내에서만 사용할 수 있었습니다. 이는 모듈 최상단에서 await을 쓸 수 없다는 문제가 존재합니다.</p>
<p>이제 <code>await</code>은 모듈 최상단에서 사용할 수 있으며, import, fallback 등을 만들 때 매우 유용합니다.</p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-top-level-await">https://github.com/tc39/proposal-top-level-await</a></p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Dynamic dependency pathing</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> strings</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`/i18n/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">navigator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">language</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Resource initialization</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> connection</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> dbConnector</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Dependency fallbacks</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> jQuery;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">try</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  jQuery </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://cdn-a.com/jQuery'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  jQuery </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://cdn-b.com/jQuery'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es2021es12">ES2021(ES12)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2021es12"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>String.prototype.replaceAll</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 현재는 전역 정규식(/regexp/g)을 사용하지 않고서는 문자열에서 부분 문자열을 대체할 수 있는 방법은 없다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fruits</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '🍎+🍐+🍓+'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fruitsWithBanana</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fruits.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">replace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#72F088;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">\+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">g</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'🍌'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(fruitsWithBanana); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//🍎🍌🍐🍌🍓🍌</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//새로 replaceAll 메서드가 String 프로토타입에 추가되었다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fruits</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '🍎+🍐+🍓+'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fruitsWithBanana</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fruits.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">replaceAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'+'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'🍌'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<ul>
<li>Promise.any</li>
</ul>
<p>프라미스 중 하나가 이행되는 즉시 응답을 준다. 단, promise 모두가 에러일 경우 AggregateError를 주게 된다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(reject, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'quick'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(resolve, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">500</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'slow'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promises</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [promise1, promise2, promise3];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(promises)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(e);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// slow</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(reject, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'quick'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(reject, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">500</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'slow'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promises</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [promise1, promise2, promise3];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(promises)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(e);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// AggregateError: All promises were rejected</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Promise.any vs Promise.race vs Promise.all vs Promise.allSettled</li>
</ul>
<p>공통점: ES2017 이후 병렬처리</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.all</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 배열 안 프라미스가 모두 처리되면 새로운 프라미스가 이행</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 결과값을 담은 배열이 새로운 프라미스가 리턴</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">all</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// [1, 2, 3]</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 프라미스가 거부되면서 Promise.all 전체가 거부되고, .catch가 실행</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">all</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'에러 발생!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Error: 에러 발생!</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.allSettled</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 프라미스가 거부되더라도 모든 프라미스가 처리될 때까지 기다립니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">allSettled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'에러 발생!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// result</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0: {status: 'fulfilled', value: 1}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1: {status: 'rejected', reason: Error: 에러 발생! at &#x3C;anonymous>:3:60 at i (https://www.notion.so/890-97af8b4e61b9aaf1ef78.js:1…}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2: {status: 'fulfilled', value: 3}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.race</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 가장 먼저 처리되는 프라미스의 결과(혹은 에러)를 반환합니다.(성공, 실패와 상관없음)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">race</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
